# 黑盒场景与操作方法

全部内容为未来执行计划，不是实际执行结果。经营参数引用已确认配置，合成数据不构成营业政策。

## BB-after-sales-1：Cancellation eligibility follows current fulfillment

需求：after-sales-1

预期结果：
Eligibility and refusal reason match each current state; submission shows processing rather than a fabricated completed cancellation; no other-customer access.

### 方法 AI-after-sales-1

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Cancellation eligibility follows current fulfillment. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

2. Create a fresh named synthetic fixture per vector through supported customer/admin/service boundaries; reopen it to verify the stated starting state. Keep unrelated control records and record before balances/statuses. Do not inspect internal code/storage or assume a particular database, API shape or worker design.

3. Resolve market, currencies/minor units, rule versions, cutoffs/inclusivity, qualifications, role permissions and operational/visual standards from supplied confirmed configuration at execution time. Compute expected totals/sets independently before action; unknown business values remain pending, never adopt fixture values as business policy. Numeric examples are synthetic probes.

4. Credentials stay in an external secure store and are referenced only; capture redacted screenshots/receiver histories. Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Owner orders in unpaid, paid/unshipped, partly shipped and delivered states; record applicable cancellation policy and another customer's order.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-1 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Owner opens cancellation for each order, submits where eligible, refreshes status; repeat with the other customer's order through its known link.

3. Assert: Eligibility and refusal reason match each current state; submission shows processing rather than a fabricated completed cancellation; no other-customer access.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Eligibility and refusal reason match each current state; submission shows processing rather than a fabricated completed cancellation; no other-customer access.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

## BB-after-sales-2：Unpaid whole-order and paid line cancellation

需求：after-sales-2

预期结果：
Unpaid order closes all lines, releases all holds once and requires new quote/order for repurchase. Paid order cancels only eligible line and creates refund pending in original mode, never claims refunded; shipped line unchanged; reason recorded.

### 方法 AI-after-sales-2

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Unpaid whole-order and paid line cancellation. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

2. Create a fresh named synthetic fixture per vector through supported customer/admin/service boundaries; reopen it to verify the stated starting state. Keep unrelated control records and record before balances/statuses. Do not inspect internal code/storage or assume a particular database, API shape or worker design.

3. Resolve market, currencies/minor units, rule versions, cutoffs/inclusivity, qualifications, role permissions and operational/visual standards from supplied confirmed configuration at execution time. Compute expected totals/sets independently before action; unknown business values remain pending, never adopt fixture values as business policy. Numeric examples are synthetic probes.

4. Credentials stay in an external secure store and are referenced only; capture redacted screenshots/receiver histories. Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Unpaid order with two lines, stock, limited promotion and points held and no payment in flight; separate paid order with one unshipped and one shipped line, in each payment mode.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-2 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Authorized staff approves cancellation, then repeats the approval; inspect order, inventory and benefits. For unpaid order attempt to buy retained goods again; for paid order cancel only eligible line.

3. Assert: Unpaid order closes all lines, releases all holds once and requires new quote/order for repurchase. Paid order cancels only eligible line and creates refund pending in original mode, never claims refunded; shipped line unchanged; reason recorded.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Unpaid order closes all lines, releases all holds once and requires new quote/order for repurchase. Paid order cancels only eligible line and creates refund pending in original mode, never claims refunded; shipped line unchanged; reason recorded.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

## BB-after-sales-3：Return, exchange and damage application data

Queryable case binds correct line, quantity, type, reason and evidence; exchange identifies target; damage identifies package. Incomplete application identifies missing required data rather than losing it or fabricating evidence.

### 方法 AI-after-sales-3

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Return, exchange and damage application data. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

5. Owner with purchased unprocessed quantities; guest ownership verified and member ownership established in separate runs. Prepare return, exchange target and damaged-package evidence, plus each required field omitted in turn.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-3 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Submit each application type through order-line after-sales entry; correct missing fields and resubmit; reopen the resulting case.

3. Assert: Queryable case binds correct line, quantity, type, reason and evidence; exchange identifies target; damage identifies package. Incomplete application identifies missing required data rather than losing it or fabricating evidence.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

## BB-after-sales-4：After-sales remaining quantity boundary

需求：after-sales-4

预期结果：
Claims cannot exceed remaining 2 individually or collectively; excess refused with available quantity; already processed units cannot be claimed again. Additional mandatory check: Both attempts refused with remaining quantity 0; no newly accepted application or reopened entitlement for already processed goods.

### 方法 AI-after-sales-4

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: After-sales remaining quantity boundary. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

2. Create a fresh named synthetic fixture per vector through supported customer/admin/service boundaries; reopen it to verify the stated starting state. Keep unrelated control records and record before balances/statuses. Do not inspect internal code/storage or assume a particular database, API shape or worker design.

3. Resolve market, currencies/minor units, rule versions, cutoffs/inclusivity, qualifications, role permissions and operational/visual standards from supplied confirmed configuration at execution time. Compute expected totals/sets independently before action; unknown business values remain pending, never adopt fixture values as business policy. Numeric examples are synthetic probes.

4. Credentials stay in an external secure store and are referenced only; capture redacted screenshots/receiver histories. Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Order line bought quantity 3 with quantity 1 already processed; remaining 2. Reset separate runs for claims of 1, 2 and 3; concurrent claims of 2 against same remaining quantity.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Additional fresh fixture: bought 3, all 3 already processed, remaining eligible quantity exactly 0; separate return and exchange attempts.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-4 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Owner submits return/exchange requests at each quantity and in two sessions concurrently; reload cases and remaining entitlement.

3. Assert: Claims cannot exceed remaining 2 individually or collectively; excess refused with available quantity; already processed units cannot be claimed again.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Owner submits quantity 1 against the fully processed line through actual application submission, then repeats from a second session; inspect cases and remaining entitlement. Assert: Both attempts refused with remaining quantity 0; no newly accepted application or reopened entitlement for already processed goods.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Claims cannot exceed remaining 2 individually or collectively; excess refused with available quantity; already processed units cannot be claimed again.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Before record proving all 3 processed, actual positive-quantity request/refusal and subsequent case list showing no accepted new claim.

## BB-after-sales-5：Original return-policy time and condition boundary

## BB-after-sales-7：Received goods inspection stays bound to item

需求：after-sales-7

预期结果：
Received serial/line, condition, evidence and refund/exchange conclusion persist on correct case; mere shipment is not represented as inspected receipt; second case remains unchanged. Additional mandatory check: Lack of a serial number does not bypass inspection: no inspection-dependent progression before inspection; order-line identity, condition, evidence and conclusion persist on the correct accessory case after inspection.

### 方法 AI-after-sales-7

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Received goods inspection stays bound to item. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

2. Create a fresh named synthetic fixture per vector through supported customer/admin/service boundaries; reopen it to verify the stated starting state. Keep unrelated control records and record before balances/statuses. Do not inspect internal code/storage or assume a particular database, API shape or worker design.

3. Resolve market, currencies/minor units, rule versions, cutoffs/inclusivity, qualifications, role permissions and operational/visual standards from supplied confirmed configuration at execution time. Compute expected totals/sets independently before action; unknown business values remain pending, never adopt fixture values as business policy. Numeric examples are synthetic probes.

4. Credentials stay in an external secure store and are referenced only; capture redacted screenshots/receiver histories. Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Two cases with distinct order lines and bicycle serial numbers; one item physically received, the other only in transit; condition and inspection evidence recorded in fixture.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Add nonserialized accessory return and exchange cases, identified solely by exact order line and quantity; each has received-but-uninspected and inspected pass/fail branches, plus an unrelated accessory line.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-7 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Receiving staff records actual receipt and inspection of first item with pass/fail conclusions in separate runs; inspect both cases.

3. Assert: Received serial/line, condition, evidence and refund/exchange conclusion persist on correct case; mere shipment is not represented as inspected receipt; second case remains unchanged.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Staff records actual accessory receipt, attempts refund/exchange progression before inspection, then records condition, inspection evidence and conclusion without any serial number; reopen both lines. Assert: Lack of a serial number does not bypass inspection: no inspection-dependent progression before inspection; order-line identity, condition, evidence and conclusion persist on the correct accessory case after inspection.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Received serial/line, condition, evidence and refund/exchange conclusion persist on correct case; mere shipment is not represented as inspected receipt; second case remains unchanged.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Accessory line IDs, absent serial field, pre-inspection rejection and saved line-based inspection/conclusion; unrelated line unchanged.

## BB-after-sales-8：Refund amount limits per unit, line, order and top-up

需求：after-sales-8

预期结果：
Approval records amount basis, currency, mode and approver; approved amount never exceeds eligible unprocessed quantity or any remaining limit; top-up balance belongs only to its case; current prices cannot inflate original refund.

## BB-after-sales-18：Every allowed after-sales transition and rejected shortcut

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Fresh case per source-state/edge in confirmed after-sales-18 table, including all six exchange pre-shipment termination states. Required payment, inspection, original-item custody and fulfillment evidence can be made satisfied or independently unsatisfied.

Named-gap repair: derive valid request shapes from the documented external contract or an observed authorized action on a separate eligible control case; prepare a fresh target case in each forbidden source state. No invented endpoint or internal store mutation.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-18 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. For EVERY listed arrow, staff performs its named business action with prerequisites satisfied and reads persisted state/history; repeat with each prerequisite missing. For each source state request every unlisted target offered by UI or supported boundary; use no guessed internal endpoint.

3. Assert: Exactly listed transitions with prerequisites succeed; skipped review/inspection, unfinished refund, undelivered replacement and unresolved original-item custody cannot reach terminal state. Source and time persist; An actual forbidden transition request must be rejected with unchanged persisted case state; absence of a UI control alone is not rejection evidence.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

For every forbidden edge in the existing transition matrix, actually submit the corresponding transition command to the supported authenticated external boundary even when its control is hidden (use captured legitimate action shape with target case identity). Capture refusal and reload state/history. A successful eligible control establishes that refusal was not merely malformed syntax. If no supported command can be exercised, record the edge unproven; never equate no control with rejection.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Exactly listed transitions with prerequisites succeed; skipped review/inspection, unfinished refund, undelivered replacement and unresolved original-item custody cannot reach terminal state. Source and time persist; An actual forbidden transition request must be rejected with unchanged persisted case state; absence of a UI control alone is not rejection evidence.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

Per forbidden edge: eligible control request success, actual forbidden request and refusal, unchanged target state/history; otherwise explicit unproven record.

## BB-after-sales-24：Completion checks the actual promised result

需求：after-sales-24

预期结果：
Unpaid cancellation needs all lines closed/stock released; refund needs all sources successful; exchange needs replacement delivered; repair needs fulfilled return/disposition; unmet conditions refuse completion and preserve pending state. Additional mandatory check: Each independently absent completion prerequisite alone prevents completed status. Restoring the last missing prerequisite permits the corresponding valid conclusion; a guard checked only in combination with another missing guard fails.

### 方法 AI-after-sales-24

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Completion checks the actual promised result. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

2. Create a fresh named synthetic fixture per vector through supported customer/admin/service boundaries; reopen it to verify the stated starting state. Keep unrelated control records and record before balances/statuses. Do not inspect internal code/storage or assume a particular database, API shape or worker design.

3. Resolve market, currencies/minor units, rule versions, cutoffs/inclusivity, qualifications, role permissions and operational/visual standards from supplied confirmed configuration at execution time. Compute expected totals/sets independently before action; unknown business values remain pending, never adopt fixture values as business policy. Numeric examples are synthetic probes.

4. Credentials stay in an external secure store and are referenced only; capture redacted screenshots/receiver histories. Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Cases for unpaid cancellation, paid cancellation, return refund, exchange, warranty and repair. For each prepare fulfilled result and one unmet completion condition in table, including unsettled received original.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Mandatory independent negative matrix from a fully satisfied control per conclusion: unpaid cancellation (one line still open; separately one stock hold not released); paid cancellation and return refund (each source in turn pending, failed or unknown, all other sources successful); exchange (replacement not delivered); warranty and repair each (result missing with handback fulfilled; separately result recorded with handback/disposition unfulfilled). For EVERY applicable conclusion also withhold received-original disposition alone while all money and other completion conditions pass. Use reachable workflow checkpoints or an authorized isolated fixture facility; if a requested state cannot be produced, mark that vector blocked, never combine missing guards to disguise it.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-24 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Staff attempts Mark complete in both branches and customer reopens case.

3. Assert: Unpaid cancellation needs all lines closed/stock released; refund needs all sources successful; exchange needs replacement delivered; repair needs fulfilled return/disposition; unmet conditions refuse completion and preserve pending state.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): At every independent negative checkpoint send the actual Complete action and reload customer/staff case. Restore only that missing condition, repeat Complete and verify positive control. Enumerate each source separately for multi-source refunds. Assert: Each independently absent completion prerequisite alone prevents completed status. Restoring the last missing prerequisite permits the corresponding valid conclusion; a guard checked only in combination with another missing guard fails.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Unpaid cancellation needs all lines closed/stock released; refund needs all sources successful; exchange needs replacement delivered; repair needs fulfilled return/disposition; unmet conditions refuse completion and preserve pending state.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Per-conclusion/per-guard matrix showing all other predicates satisfied, exact Complete request/result, refused persisted state and restored-condition positive result.

## BB-after-sales-27：Pre-shipment exchange termination and settlement

需求：after-sales-27

预期结果：
Target hold releases once; reason/request time/responsible person retained; unrefunded top-up linked to case and original transaction becomes pending refund. Only never-sent original with no refund due can terminate immediately; other cases await settlement; shipped case cannot use this pre-shipment route. Additional mandatory check: Outstanding obligation is C-R=70, never C; previously refunded 30 is not generated again. With H=20 already represented, at most 50 becomes a new reservation/task for the same obligation; total outstanding remains 70. Repeated withdrawal does not add amounts; aggregate successful refunds cannot exceed 100.

### 方法 AI-after-sales-27

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Pre-shipment exchange termination and settlement. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Use separate reachable branches for customer withdrawal and inability to fulfill: (N) original never sent, no inspection/target reservation/top-up; (T) original inbound in transit, no inspection/target reservation/top-up; (R) original received and inspected, target held where acquired, confirmed top-up absent or present. Replacement unshipped in N/T/R. Top-up and reserved-target branches run only after inspection in R, never crossed into N/T. Include separate already-shipped replacement control.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Add received-and-inspected original with confirmed top-up C=100 synthetic minor units, already-successfully-refunded R=30; no other obligation in first run. Second run also has pending refund reservation H=20 belonging to this same top-up obligation. Replacement unshipped. Run customer withdrawal and confirmed inability to fulfill separately.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-27 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Customer withdraws or staff confirms failure with reason; inspect holds, original disposition, top-up refund task and customer status; repeat action.

3. Assert: Target hold releases once; reason/request time/responsible person retained; unrefunded top-up linked to case and original transaction becomes pending refund. Only never-sent original with no refund due can terminate immediately; other cases await settlement; shipped case cannot use this pre-shipment route.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Withdraw/terminate, repeat same action, open every source-linked refund task/reservation and receiver history. Complete the existing H task in second run, then settle remaining tasks; compare total refunds to original C. Assert: Outstanding obligation is C-R=70, never C; previously refunded 30 is not generated again. With H=20 already represented, at most 50 becomes a new reservation/task for the same obligation; total outstanding remains 70. Repeated withdrawal does not add amounts; aggregate successful refunds cannot exceed 100.

**未来执行证据**

Only never-sent original with no refund due can terminate immediately; other cases await settlement; shipped case cannot use this pre-shipment route.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

## BB-after-sales-32：Refund source reservations cannot exceed or cross balances

需求：after-sales-32

预期结果：
Each allocation binds eligible confirmed source and reserves once; aggregate accepted reservations never exceed that source balance; cross-source or mode borrowing and unconfirmed top-up refused with basis saved. Additional mandatory check: No partially completed approval is usable to execute an incompletely reserved allocation; funds represented by any recoverable in-flight reservation cannot be reused by competing approval. Recovery yields either no approved allocation or the complete single approval, with each source reserve exact once; no leaked/doubled reserve and no source overspend.

### 方法 AI-after-sales-32

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Refund source reservations cannot exceed or cross balances. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

2. Create a fresh named synthetic fixture per vector through supported customer/admin/service boundaries; reopen it to verify the stated starting state. Keep unrelated control records and record before balances/statuses. Do not inspect internal code/storage or assume a particular database, API shape or worker design.

3. Resolve market, currencies/minor units, rule versions, cutoffs/inclusivity, qualifications, role permissions and operational/visual standards from supplied confirmed configuration at execution time. Compute expected totals/sets independently before action; unknown business values remain pending, never adopt fixture values as business policy. Numeric examples are synthetic probes.

4. Credentials stay in an external secure store and are referenced only; capture redacted screenshots/receiver histories. Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Original payment and two confirmed top-ups with different cases/modes; one unconfirmed top-up. Record each received amount minus successful refunds and other pending reservations; two approvals race for last refundable minor unit.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Add one approval allocating refunds across original payment and confirmed case top-up, each with independently recorded remaining balance; run real-contract and test pools separately. Use an authorized isolated fault facility capable of acknowledging a business-write attempt in flight and causing persistence rejection or process interruption before that operation finishes. Record the actual reached phase/fault acknowledgement without prescribing storage technology or inspecting implementation. A socket response drop or uncorrelated restart does NOT satisfy this vector. If no such controllable phase can be reached and evidenced, keep it unproven/blocked.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-32 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Approve exact balance, then over by one minor unit, duplicate approval identifier, concurrent separate approvals and cross-case/transaction/mode transfers.

3. Assert: Each allocation binds eligible confirmed source and reserves once; aggregate accepted reservations never exceed that source balance; cross-source or mode borrowing and unconfirmed top-up refused with basis saved.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Staff submits multi-source approval. Inject persistence failure after one source reservation has been attempted but before the remaining allocation/approval completion; separately interrupt after reserve attempt before acknowledgement. During recovery submit a competing approval against affected balance, then retry SAME original approval ID after restoring service. Assert: No partially completed approval is usable to execute an incompletely reserved allocation; funds represented by any recoverable in-flight reservation cannot be reused by competing approval. Recovery yields either no approved allocation or the complete single approval, with each source reserve exact once; no leaked/doubled reserve and no source overspend.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Each allocation binds eligible confirmed source and reserves once; aggregate accepted reservations never exceed that source balance; cross-source or mode borrowing and unconfirmed top-up refused with basis saved.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Correlated approval request, confirmed mid-reservation fault, before/during/after per-source externally visible reserves, competing approval result and final same-ID retry allocation. Normal shortage alone is not evidence.

## BB-cart-checkout-14：Successful payment confirms money and used benefits once

需求：cart-checkout-14

预期结果：
Order becomes paid with correct mode/confirmed amount/currency/reference; only used holds convert once, no partial visible state where paid order retains unconfirmed used benefit; test money not real receipts. Additional mandatory check: No partially applied paid state is exposed as completed/fulfillable while used benefits or confirmed amount/reference disagree. State is either consistent pre-application/pending reconciliation or consistently completed; recovery applies original success once with exact money reference and each used benefit once. Partial committed effects left inconsistent after interruption fail.

Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Add receiver-verified successful payment with BOTH promo and points holds valid; repeat each optional-benefit mode where applicable. Use an authorized isolated fault facility capable of acknowledging a business-write attempt in flight and causing persistence rejection or process interruption before that operation finishes. Record the actual reached phase/fault acknowledgement without prescribing storage technology or inspecting implementation. A socket response drop or uncorrelated restart does NOT satisfy this vector. If no such controllable phase can be reached and evidenced, keep it unproven/blocked.

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) cart-checkout-14 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Customer completes payment, staff reconciles result and repeats result delivery; inspect order and both benefit histories together.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Apply the success result through actual reconciliation/receiver boundary. Separately interrupt while order-paid update, payment-reference persistence, promo use or points use is attempted before overall success confirmation. After fault inspect owner/staff order and benefit views, recover, replay original success and reconcile again without another debit. Assert: No partially applied paid state is exposed as completed/fulfillable while used benefits or confirmed amount/reference disagree. State is either consistent pre-application/pending reconciliation or consistently completed; recovery applies original success once with exact money reference and each used benefit once. Partial committed effects left inconsistent after interruption fail.

## BB-cart-checkout-22：Order and all required holds succeed or fail together

需求：cart-checkout-22

预期结果：
Success saves snapshot and same-mode holds together. Any required failure produces no payable order or payment and no orphan holds, identifies affected line/reason, never borrows live resources for test. Additional mandatory check: Failed/interrupted creation never exposes a payable partial order or initiates payment. A rejected submission leaves no leaked partial holds; a recoverable in-flight submission is visibly nonpayable until it resolves completely. Retry yields at most one complete order/snapshot with exactly one set of mode-correct holds, or clean failure with no holds.

### 方法 AI-cart-checkout-22

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Order and all required holds succeed or fail together. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

3. Resolve market, currencies/minor units, rule versions, cutoffs/inclusivity, qualifications, role permissions and operational/visual standards from supplied confirmed configuration at execution time. Compute expected totals/sets independently before action; unknown business values remain pending, never adopt fixture values as business policy. Numeric examples are synthetic probes.

4. Credentials stay in an external secure store and are referenced only; capture redacted screenshots/receiver histories. Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Real/test cart with two lines and none/promo/points/both benefits. Force shortage independently in stock, promo, points, and test resource; one real line unready. Separate all-ready fixture.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Add fully qualified quote with sufficient stock, limited promo and points so no validation shortage masks persistence behavior. Use an authorized isolated fault facility capable of acknowledging a business-write attempt in flight and causing persistence rejection or process interruption before that operation finishes. Record the actual reached phase/fault acknowledgement without prescribing storage technology or inspecting implementation. A socket response drop or uncorrelated restart does NOT satisfy this vector. If no such controllable phase can be reached and evidenced, keep it unproven/blocked.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) cart-checkout-22 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Customer submits confirmed quote including two concurrent attempts for final resource; inspect order list, receiver attempts and all balances before/after.

3. Assert: Success saves snapshot and same-mode holds together. Any required failure produces no payable order or payment and no orphan holds, identifies affected line/reason, never borrows live resources for test.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Customer submits confirmed quote. In independent runs fail order-snapshot persistence, interrupt after stock reservation attempt before remaining holds, and fail later promo/points persistence after earlier reservation work. Restore service and retry the same submission; inspect all pools/orders/receiver attempts before and after recovery. Assert: Failed/interrupted creation never exposes a payable partial order or initiates payment. A rejected submission leaves no leaked partial holds; a recoverable in-flight submission is visibly nonpayable until it resolves completely. Retry yields at most one complete order/snapshot with exactly one set of mode-correct holds, or clean failure with no holds.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Success saves snapshot and same-mode holds together. Any required failure produces no payable order or payment and no orphan holds, identifies affected line/reason, never borrows live resources for test.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Actual submission plus acknowledged persistence failure/interruption phase, nonpayable order state, every resource balance/reservation and receiver baseline, and post-recovery same-submission result.

## BB-customer-support-7：Concurrent assignment leaves one notified owner

需求：customer-support-7

预期结果：
At each observable settled state exactly one responsible agent; notification matches effective assignment, no competing active owners. Additional mandatory check: At every observed interval there is at most one effective owner; after successful assignment there is one. Two agents must never both be authorized against the same ownership interval. Sequential A-then-B authority after an actual recorded transfer is not a false failure. Notifications correspond to ownership sequence.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Instrument external assignment acceptance/notifications and both agent sessions with a common timestamped trace. Use an authorized controllable assignment service boundary or test scheduler to hold requests in flight and release competing A/B assignments in both orders; no settled-state-only sampling.

2. Assign A/B concurrently, reload queue/detail and inspect resulting responsibility and notifications.

3. Assert: At each observable settled state exactly one responsible agent; notification matches effective assignment, no competing active owners.

After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Start both actual assignments at a barrier. At each exposed intermediate boundary, capture active ownership updates and have A/B submit uniquely marked owner-only replies while next assignment commit is held; record authorization decisions and assignment history before allowing the next phase. Repeat release order. Assert: At every observed interval there is at most one effective owner; after successful assignment there is one. Notifications correspond to ownership sequence.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Correlated in-flight assignment, ownership, notification and owner-only action request/decision timeline sufficient to detect overlapping effective ownership; if controllable observation cannot expose the race interval, mark vector unproven rather than passing on final state.

预期结果：
Authorized replies persist sender/content/time and are readable live or after re-entry; unauthorized reply cannot appear as valid assigned-agent reply. Lack of a new-session permission is not the only tested case.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Add separate real-contract paid order with three lines, all unshipped and individually cancellable; do not reuse the already-shipped fixture. Cancellation/refund states initially none.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) orders-fulfillment-18 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Authorize cancellation of first line, then second, then last, viewing customer summary and line histories each time. After all cancelled, keep refunds pending then settle them through original sources. Assert: Summary transitions partial cancellation to all lines cancelled at the last cancellation; refund pending/success remains separate. It must not remain only partly cancelled or claim all refunded before source results. Original payment/line event histories survive.

**未来执行证据**

1. For EACH vector: redacted setup/configuration and before-state record; ordered customer/staff action trace showing the actual action; final and reloaded user-visible page or supported external-system result. No execution evidence exists at design time.

2. Assertion evidence must demonstrate: Partial/all shipping, delivery and cancellation accurately derived; refund/after-sales displayed separately; earlier events and per-line reality retained.

3. Where money, quantities, notifications or repeated actions are involved: before/after scoped balances, transaction/message/record identifiers and receiver history sufficient to detect a duplicate, wrong recipient or wrong mode. A screenshot of a button click alone is insufficient.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Three authorized cancellation actions, line-state snapshots and aggregate summary after each including all-cancelled, plus separately evolving refund state.

## BB-site-quality-12：Interrupted writes and timeouts remain reconcilable

需求：site-quality-12

预期结果：
Understandable pending/failure state and reconcilable original record retained; retry does not duplicate respective business effect; unknown money result not falsely finalized. Additional mandatory check: Interrupted writes produce understandable unresolved/failure status with original correlation preserved; after recovery each operation is either unapplied/retryable or applied once consistently, never duplicate orders/charges/refunds/messages or contradictory completed money state. An untriggered write fault cannot pass.

### 方法 AI-site-quality-12

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Keep existing external-timeout vectors and add distinct actual interrupted-business-write vectors for order creation, payment-result application, refund reservation/result and message persistence. Use an authorized isolated fault facility capable of acknowledging a business-write attempt in flight and causing persistence rejection or process interruption before that operation finishes. Record the actual reached phase/fault acknowledgement without prescribing storage technology or inspecting implementation. A socket response drop or uncorrelated restart does NOT satisfy this vector. If no such controllable phase can be reached and evidenced, keep it unproven/blocked.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) site-quality-12 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Named-gap repair (mandatory additional vector): Perform each actual user/business action; interrupt its write operation before completed persistence (not just response delivery), verify fault acknowledgement, restore service and reopen/reconcile original operation. Retry through offered user path using same identity/reference; observe business records and receiver outcomes. Assert: Interrupted writes produce understandable unresolved/failure status with original correlation preserved; after recovery each operation is either unapplied/retryable or applied once consistently, never duplicate orders/charges/refunds/messages or contradictory completed money state. An untriggered write fault cannot pass.

## BB-journey-exchange-settlement-withdrawn：Exchange top-up withdrawal requires both money and custody resolution — withdrawn

需求：after-sales-27, after-sales-28, after-sales-29, after-sales-31, after-sales-32, after-sales-33, orders-fulfillment-2

预期结果：
Branch N terminates with no original-item or refund obligation. Branch T remains pending until actual original disposition is fulfilled. In R, existing target hold releases once; only unrefunded confirmed top-up obligation is represented once, existing pending allocations counted rather than duplicated; original-reference reconciliation preserves each allocation outcome and permits closure only after both all funds and original disposition settled. No branch rewrites original order snapshot; no branch simultaneously assumes unreceived and inspected original.

### 方法 AI-journey-exchange-settlement

Planned reusable AI-operated boundary procedure: Exchange top-up withdrawal requires both money and custody resolution. Re-run with fresh fixtures for every enumerated vector; not executed evidence.

**前置条件**

1. PLAN ONLY. Execute later on an available authorized app/test environment. If a required route, resource, policy, observable record or oracle is unavailable, mark the affected vector unproven/blocked; do not fabricate a result or infer approval. No app location is required during design.

2. Create a fresh named synthetic fixture per vector through supported customer/admin/service boundaries; reopen it to verify the stated starting state. Keep unrelated control records and record before balances/statuses. Do not inspect internal code/storage or assume a particular database, API shape or worker design.

3. Resolve market, currencies/minor units, rule versions, cutoffs/inclusivity, qualifications, role permissions and operational/visual standards from supplied confirmed configuration at execution time. Compute expected totals/sets independently before action; unknown business values remain pending, never adopt fixture values as business policy. Numeric examples are synthetic probes.

4. Credentials stay in an external secure store and are referenced only; capture redacted screenshots/receiver histories. Real-mode assertions use an authorized real-contract test environment and separately verified integration resources when later available; isolated app test mode is a distinct branch and does not prove real integration. Never initiate unapproved production charges, shipping or restore operations.

5. Three DISJOINT reachable branches; each begins with a paid original order and saved original snapshot, replacement never shipped. N: approved exchange, original never sent, no receipt/inspection, no target hold, no top-up. T: approved exchange, original inbound in transit, no receipt/inspection, no target hold, no top-up. R: original actually received and inspected, approved target hold acquired, positive difference confirmed by customer and top-up accepted; subcases C=100 with Rpaid=0 or already refunded Rpaid=30 synthetic minor units, and optional existing pending same-source refund reservation H=20. Never put inspected receipt or post-inspection money/stock states in N or T. Real-contract and isolated-test modes are separate executions. Refund outcomes unknown/failure/success apply only to R. If an alternative fixture cannot be reached through authorized workflow, report it blocked instead of asserting contradictory premises.

**操作步骤**

1. Verify and record branch N/T/R predicates and original order snapshot from owner/staff boundaries before acting.
2. In N customer withdraws (repeat once); verify immediate termination with no invented hold/refund. In T customer withdraws; verify waiting for termination settlement, then trace actual original receipt and fulfill applicable return/disposition before terminal closure; no invented inspection/top-up at starting state.
3. In R, create confirmed top-up through real customer difference confirmation (repeat same confirmation to challenge duplicate debit), then establish any specified prior partial refund and H via actual approved refund actions. Withdraw before replacement dispatch, repeat withdrawal and inspect target release and source-linked outstanding obligation C-Rpaid. Existing H is part of that obligation, not added again; at most C-Rpaid-H newly reserved for this same obligation.
4. Only in R, execute/reconcile original-reference refund with unknown, failed and successful outcomes in fresh runs; retain the existing source allocations and compare prior partial refund with settlement of the remaining approved obligation, without inventing an additional top-up attempt. Unknown retains reservation and cannot be reissued; failed allocation requires reapproval; preserve successful amounts.
5. In separate R runs fulfill only original disposition first or only all money first, attempt terminal closure while the other obligation remains, then fulfill the other. Read customer custody/funds history and compare immutable original snapshot. Across all branches keep branch-specific assertions; money assertions do not pretend to execute in N/T.
6. Assert: Branch N terminates with no original-item or refund obligation. Branch T remains pending until actual original disposition is fulfilled. In R, existing target hold releases once; only unrefunded confirmed top-up obligation is represented once, existing pending allocations counted rather than duplicated; original-reference reconciliation preserves each allocation outcome and permits closure only after both all funds and original disposition settled. No branch rewrites original order snapshot; no branch simultaneously assumes unreceived and inspected original.

**未来执行证据**

Branch-labeled reachable setup chronology and customer/staff actions; N no-obligation terminal result; T transit-to-fulfilled-disposition pending/terminal record; R confirmed top-up, prior refunds, reservation/task/source amounts, actual receiver fault/reconciliation traces and both one-obligation-unresolved closure rejections. Before/after original snapshot in every branch. All are planned evidence, not execution claims. Branch N terminates with no original-item or refund obligation. Branch T remains pending until actual original disposition is fulfilled. In R, existing target hold releases once; only unrefunded confirmed top-up obligation is represented once, existing pending allocations counted rather than duplicated; original-reference reconciliation preserves each allocation outcome and permits closure only after both all funds and original disposition settled. No branch rewrites original order snapshot; no branch simultaneously assumes unreceived and inspected original.

**操作步骤**

1. Read the confirmed requirement(s) after-sales-8, after-sales-9, after-sales-10, after-sales-19, after-sales-25, after-sales-32, after-sales-33 from requirements.json. Enumerate every setup vector, including all stated table rows and independent omissions; record concrete fixture IDs, role, mode and expected oracle before execution.

2. Execute all allocations with first success, second unknown and third failure. Attempt whole-case re-execution and overlapping approval. Reconcile unknown to success, reapprove failed allocation and retry it to success; replay all results.

3. Assert: Successful source recorded once, unknown source reserve retained, failed reserve released; overlap cannot reuse reserved amount; case remains unknown then failed as precedence requires. Only reapproved failed source is retried; case complete only all verified success, source balances and refunded total equal approval exactly with no duplicate money action.

4. After each vector, reload/re-enter through the appropriate authorized user or external receiver boundary and compare the observed state with the independently recorded before state and expected result. Check named unrelated control records. Record failures and missing oracles per vector; resetting one vector must not mask an earlier failure.

Only reapproved failed source is retried; case complete only all verified success, source balances and refunded total equal approval exactly with no duplicate money action.
